TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LSTM的深度学习框架，用于校准低成本空气质量传感器（LCS）。该方法在PM$_{2.5}$、PM$_{10}$和NO$_{2}$的监测上优于随机森林基线模型，并达到了监管合规标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本空气质量传感器（LCS）为构建密集的城市监测网络提供了可行的替代方案，但其应用受到校准挑战的限制，包括传感器漂移、环境交叉敏感性以及设备间的性能差异。

Method: 开发了一个基于长短期记忆网络（LSTM）的深度学习框架，利用英国牛津OxAria网络的共置参考数据进行训练。该方法通过基于序列的学习捕捉时间依赖性和延迟的环境效应。特征集结合了时间滞后参数、谐波编码和交互项以提高泛化能力。

Result: 该方法在三种污染物的训练、验证和测试集中均获得了比随机森林基线模型更高的$R^2$值。验证结果表明，校准后的数值符合监管合规要求，NO$_{2}$、PM$_{10}$和PM$_{2.5}$的扩展不确定度分别为22.11%、12.42%和9.1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的LSTM框架成功解决了LCS的校准挑战，实现了符合监管标准的测量精度，证明了深度学习方法在低成本空气质量传感器校准中的有效性，有助于推动密集城市监测网络的部署。

Abstract: Low-cost air quality sensors (LCS) provide a practical alternative to expensive regulatory-grade instruments, making dense urban monitoring networks possible. Yet their adoption is limited by calibration challenges, including sensor drift, environmental cross-sensitivity, and variability in performance from device to device. This work presents a deep learning framework for calibrating LCS measurements of PM$_{2.5}$, PM$_{10}$, and NO$_2$ using a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network, trained on co-located reference data from the OxAria network in Oxford, UK. Unlike the Random Forest (RF) baseline, which treats each observation independently, the proposed approach captures temporal dependencies and delayed environmental effects through sequence-based learning, achieving higher $R^2$ values across training, validation, and test sets for all three pollutants. A feature set is constructed combining time-lagged parameters, harmonic encodings, and interaction terms to improve generalization on unseen temporal windows. Validation of unseen calibrated values against the Equivalence Spreadsheet Tool 3.1 demonstrates regulatory compliance with expanded uncertainties of 22.11% for NO$_2$, 12.42% for PM$_{10}$, and 9.1% for PM$_{2.5}$.

</details>


### [60] [Hybrid Deep Learning Approach for Coupled Demand Forecasting and Supply Chain Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21567)
*Nusrat Yasmin Nadia,Md Habibul Arif,Habibor Rahman Rabby,Md Iftekhar Monzur Tanvir,Md. Jakir Hossen,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个混合AI框架（HAF-DS），通过整合LSTM需求预测与MILP优化层，显著提升了纺织和PPE供应链的预测准确性与运营效率，实现了库存成本降低和服务水平提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纺织和个人防护装备（PPE）等行业面临需求波动大、供应不确定的挑战，而传统的预测与优化方法通常孤立运作，限制了其在实际供应链中的效果，因此需要一种协同预测与优化的综合解决方案。

Method: 提出了需求-供应预测与优化混合AI框架（HAF-DS）。该框架集成了基于长短期记忆网络（LSTM）的需求预测模块与混合整数线性规划（MILP）优化层；LSTM负责捕捉时间序列和上下文需求依赖关系，优化层则制定成本效益最优的补货与分配决策，通过嵌入式特征表示和循环神经架构联合最小化预测误差与运营成本。

Result: 在纺织销售与供应链数据集上的实验表明，HAF-DS显著优于统计和深度学习基线。在组合数据集上，MAE从15.04降至12.83（降低14.7%），RMSE从19.53降至17.11（降低12.4%），MAPE从9.5%降至8.1%；同时库存成本降低5.4%，缺货率降低27.5%，服务水平从95.5%提升至97.8%。

Conclusion: 将预测性预测与规范性优化相结合，能够同时提升预测准确性与运营效率，为现代纺织和PPE供应链提供了一个可扩展且适应性强的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Supply chain resilience and efficiency are vital in industries characterized by volatile demand and uncertain supply, such as textiles and personal protective equipment (PPE). Traditional forecasting and optimization approaches often operate in isolation, limiting their real-world effectiveness. This paper proposes a Hybrid AI Framework for Demand-Supply Forecasting and Optimization (HAF-DS), which integrates a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM)-based demand forecasting module with a mixed integer linear programming (MILP) optimization layer. The LSTM captures temporal and contextual demand dependencies, while the optimization layer prescribes cost-efficient replenishment and allocation decisions. The framework jointly minimizes forecasting error and operational cost through embedding-based feature representation and recurrent neural architectures. Experiments on textile sales and supply chain datasets show significant performance gains over statistical and deep learning baselines. On the combined dataset, HAF-DS reduced Mean Absolute Error (MAE) from 15.04 to 12.83 (14.7%), Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) from 19.53 to 17.11 (12.4%), and Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) from 9.5% to 8.1%. Inventory cost decreased by 5.4%, stockouts by 27.5%, and service level rose from 95.5% to 97.8%. These results confirm that coupling predictive forecasting with prescriptive optimization enhances both accuracy and efficiency, providing a scalable and adaptable solution for modern textile and PPE supply chains.

</details>


### [61] [Geometric Characterisation and Structured Trajectory Surrogates for Clinical Dataset Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21638)
*Pafue Christy Nganjimi,Andrew Soltan,Danielle Belgrave,Lei Clifton,David Clifton,Anshul Thakur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝塞尔轨迹匹配（BTM）方法，通过用二次贝塞尔曲线替代SGD轨迹来解决传统轨迹匹配中的条件可表示性瓶颈，在临床数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹匹配（TM）是一种广泛使用的数据集蒸馏方法，但其监督信号的结构尚未被充分理解。本文发现固定的合成数据集只能复现有限范围的训练诱导参数变化，当监督信号频谱较宽时，会产生条件可表示性瓶颈，即合成数据集无法有效表示复杂的SGD轨迹。

Method: 提出贝塞尔轨迹匹配（BTM）方法，用连接初始和最终模型状态的二次贝塞尔轨迹替代SGD轨迹。这些代理轨迹被优化以降低沿路径的平均损失，同时用更结构化、低秩的信号替代宽泛的SGD监督信号，使其更好地与固定合成数据集的优化约束对齐，并大幅减少轨迹存储需求。

Result: 在五个临床数据集上的实验表明，BTM始终匹配或优于标准轨迹匹配方法，在低患病率和低合成预算设置中获得最大收益。结果表明有效的轨迹匹配依赖于构建监督信号，而非复现随机优化路径。

Conclusion: 本研究为轨迹匹配提供了几何表征，揭示了条件可表示性瓶颈的存在，并提出了一种有效的解决方案。核心洞察是：有效的轨迹匹配应关注监督信号的结构化，而非简单复现SGD的随机优化路径，这为数据集蒸馏方法的设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Dataset condensation constructs compact synthetic datasets that retain the training utility of large real-world datasets, enabling efficient model development and potentially supporting downstream research in governed domains such as healthcare. Trajectory matching (TM) is a widely used condensation approach that supervises synthetic data using changes in model parameters observed during training on real data, yet the structure of this supervision signal remains poorly understood. In this paper, we provide a geometric characterisation of trajectory matching, showing that a fixed synthetic dataset can only reproduce a limited span of such training-induced parameter changes. When the resulting supervision signal is spectrally broad, this creates a conditional representability bottleneck. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose Bezier Trajectory Matching (BTM), which replaces SGD trajectories with quadratic Bezier trajectory surrogates between initial and final model states. These surrogates are optimised to reduce average loss along the path while replacing broad SGD-derived supervision with a more structured, lower-rank signal that is better aligned with the optimisation constraints of a fixed synthetic dataset, and they substantially reduce trajectory storage. Experiments on five clinical datasets demonstrate that BTM consistently matches or improves upon standard trajectory matching, with the largest gains in low-prevalence and low-synthetic-budget settings. These results indicate that effective trajectory matching depends on structuring the supervision signal rather than reproducing stochastic optimisation paths.

</details>


### [62] [Task-specific Subnetwork Discovery in Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Underwater Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21640)
*Yi-Ling Liu,Melvin Laux,Mariela De Lucas Alvarez,Frank Kirchner,Rebecca Adam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析水下自主航行器的多任务强化学习网络内部结构，发现网络仅需约1.5%的权重即可区分不同任务，其中约85%连接情境变量节点，揭示了情境变量的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务强化学习策略虽然在水下导航等复杂任务中表现出色，但其内部决策过程不透明，缺乏可解释性，导致透明度、信任度和安全性问题，阻碍了实际部署。现有研究对策略内部结构和任务特化机制理解不足。

Method: 在HoloOcean模拟器中，对预训练的多任务强化学习网络进行分析，识别并比较负责导航至不同目标的任务特定子网络。通过结构分析确定区分任务的权重分布及其连接模式。

Result: 在相关任务的多任务强化学习设置中，网络仅需约1.5%的权重来区分不同任务。在这些权重中，约85%连接输入层的情境变量节点到下一隐藏层，凸显了情境变量在此类设置中的重要性。

Conclusion: 该方法揭示了网络中的共享组件和专用组件，为模型编辑、迁移学习和持续学习提供了有效途径，有助于通过情境多任务强化学习方法实现高效的水下监测。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles are required to perform multiple tasks adaptively and in an explainable manner under dynamic, uncertain conditions and limited sensing, challenges that classical controllers struggle to address. This demands robust, generalizable, and inherently interpretable control policies for reliable long-term monitoring. Reinforcement learning, particularly multi-task RL, overcomes these limitations by leveraging shared representations to enable efficient adaptation across tasks and environments. However, while such policies show promising results in simulation and controlled experiments, they yet remain opaque and offer limited insight into the agent's internal decision-making, creating gaps in transparency, trust, and safety that hinder real-world deployment. The internal policy structure and task-specific specialization remain poorly understood. To address these gaps, we analyze the internal structure of a pretrained multi-task reinforcement learning network in the HoloOcean simulator for underwater navigation by identifying and comparing task-specific subnetworks responsible for navigating toward different species. We find that in a contextual multi-task reinforcement learning setting with related tasks, the network uses only about 1.5% of its weights to differentiate between tasks. Of these, approximately 85% connect the context-variable nodes in the input layer to the next hidden layer, highlighting the importance of context variables in such settings. Our approach provides insights into shared and specialized network components, useful for efficient model editing, transfer learning, and continual learning for underwater monitoring through a contextual multi-task reinforcement learning method.

</details>


### [63] [Large-Scale Data Parallelization of Product Quantization and Inverted Indexing Using Dask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21645)
*Ashley N. Abraham,Andrew Strelzoff,Haley R. Dozier,Althea C. Henslee,Mark A. Chappell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合乘积量化(PQ)、倒排索引和Dask的分布式近似最近邻搜索方法，通过分治策略处理大规模数据，在保持准确率的同时将计算需求降低到中等规模数据的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模最近邻搜索虽然应用广泛，但面临处理大规模数据时计算限制的挑战。虽然近似最近邻(ANN)搜索可以降低计算开销，但聚类大规模高维数据仍然需要沉重的内存和时间成本，亟需一种既能处理大规模数据又能控制计算资源消耗的方法。

Method: 采用分治策略，结合乘积量化(PQ)进行内存高效的近似最近邻搜索，利用倒排索引加速检索过程，并使用Dask分布式计算框架在Python环境中实现大规模数据的并行处理，最后合并各分区结果。

Result: 该方法成功处理大规模数据，在不牺牲准确率的前提下，将计算需求降低至处理中等规模数据所需的水平，有效降低了内存成本和执行时间。

Conclusion: 通过PQ、倒排索引和Dask的组合，实现了对大规模数据的高效分治处理，为大规模近似最近邻搜索提供了一种在计算资源和准确率之间取得良好平衡的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale Nearest Neighbor (NN) search, though widely utilized in the similarity search field, remains challenged by the computational limitations inherent in processing large scale data. In an effort to decrease the computational expense needed, Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search is often used in applications that do not require the exact similarity search, but instead can rely on an approximation. Product Quantization (PQ) is a memory-efficient ANN effective for clustering all sizes of datasets. Clustering large-scale, high dimensional data requires a heavy computational expense, in both memory-cost and execution time. This work focuses on a unique way to divide and conquer the large scale data in Python using PQ, Inverted Indexing and Dask, combining the results without compromising the accuracy and reducing computational requirements to the level required when using medium-scale data.

</details>


### [64] [Dilated CNNs for Periodic Signal Processing: A Low-Complexity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21651)
*Eli Gildish,Michael Grebshtein,Igor Makienko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为R-DCNN的低计算复杂度方法，通过结合深度卷积神经网络（DCNN）与重采样技术，在仅需单次观测训练的条件下实现了周期信号的高效去噪与波形估计，性能媲美现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习方法在周期信号去噪和波形估计任务中表现优异，但存在计算资源消耗大、需针对每次观测单独训练的问题，难以应用于功率和资源受限的环境。

Method: 提出R-DCNN方法，利用重采样步骤对不同基频的信号进行时间尺度对齐，从而复用同一组网络权重，实现仅需单次观测训练即可泛化至其他信号，大幅降低计算开销。

Result: R-DCNN在保持低计算复杂度的同时，达到了与自回归（AR）等经典先进方法以及针对单次观测单独训练的传统DCNN相当的去噪与估计性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功兼顾了计算效率与性能，非常适合部署在资源受限的场景中，在不牺牲去噪或估计精度的前提下解决了传统深度学习方法的高资源消耗问题。

Abstract: Denoising of periodic signals and accurate waveform estimation are core tasks across many signal processing domains, including speech, music, medical diagnostics, radio, and sonar. Although deep learning methods have recently shown performance improvements over classical approaches, they require substantial computational resources and are usually trained separately for each signal observation. This study proposes a computationally efficient method based on DCNN and Re-sampling, termed R-DCNN, designed for operation under strict power and resource constraints. The approach targets signals with varying fundamental frequencies and requires only a single observation for training. It generalizes to additional signals via a lightweight resampling step that aligns time scales in signals with different frequencies to re-use the same network weights. Despite its low computational complexity, R-DCNN achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art classical methods, such as autoregressive (AR)-based techniques, as well as conventional DCNNs trained individually for each observation. This combination of efficiency and performance makes the proposed method particularly well suited for deployment in resource-constrained environments without sacrificing denoising or estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [65] [Transferable SCF-Acceleration through Solver-Aligned Initialization Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21657)
*Eike S. Eberhard,Viktor Kotsev,Timm Güthle,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAIL通过端到端微分SCF求解器解决了机器学习初始猜测在外推大分子时收敛变慢的问题，在大分子上实现了显著的收敛加速和实际计算时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分子几何预测初始猜测的机器学习方法在外推到更大分子时会失效，反而降低收敛速度。本文指出这一失败源于监督问题而非外推问题：模型在分布外能很好拟合基态目标，但生成的初始猜测会拖慢收敛。

Method: 提出Solver-Aligned Initialization Learning (SAIL)方法，通过对SCF求解器进行端到端微分来解决Hamiltonian和密度矩阵模型的监督问题。引入Effective Relative Iteration Count (ERIC)指标，修正了常用RIC中未考虑的Fock构建开销。

Result: 在QM40数据集上（分子比训练分布大4倍），SAIL在PBE、SCAN和B3LYP水平下分别降低ERIC 37%、33%和27%，在B3LYP上的改进幅度超过之前最佳结果（10%）的两倍。在QMugs数据集上（分子比训练尺寸大10倍），SAIL在杂化泛函水平实现了1.25倍的实际运行时间加速。

Conclusion: SAIL成功将机器学习SCF加速扩展到大尺寸类药分子，证明了通过求解器对齐的监督学习可以克服传统方法在外推时的失效问题，为大规模量子化学计算提供了有效的加速方案。

Abstract: Machine learning methods that predict initial guesses from molecular geometry can reduce this cost, but matrix-prediction models fail when extrapolating to larger molecules, degrading rather than accelerating convergence [Liu et al. 2025]. We show that this failure is a supervision problem, not an extrapolation problem: models trained on ground-state targets fit those targets well out of distribution, yet produce initial guesses that slow convergence. Solver-Aligned Initialization Learning (SAIL) resolves this for both Hamiltonian and density matrix models by differentiating through the SCF solver end-to-end. We introduce the Effective Relative Iteration Count (ERIC), a correction to the commonly used RIC that accounts for hidden Fock-build overhead. On QM40, containing molecules up to 4$\times$ larger than the training distribution, SAIL reduces ERIC by 37% (PBE), 33% (SCAN), and 27% (B3LYP), more than doubling the previous state-of-the-art reduction on B3LYP (10%). On QMugs molecules 10$\times$ the training size, SAIL delivers a 1.25$\times$ wall-time speedup at the hybrid level of theory, extending ML SCF acceleration to large drug-like molecules.

</details>


### [66] [Geometric Monomial (GEM): a family of rational 2N-differentiable activation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21677)
*Eylon E. Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEM系列平滑激活函数，在CNN和Transformer上达到或超越GELU性能，并发现了平滑度参数N在CNN和Transformer间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReLU虽为主流激活函数，但其非平滑性可能阻碍深度架构中基于梯度的优化，因此需要一种既保持ReLU性能又具备平滑特性的激活函数。

Method: 提出基于log-logistic CDF门控机制的$C^{2N}$平滑激活函数族，包含三个变体：GEM（基础族）、E-GEM（带$ε$参数化实现任意$L^p$逼近ReLU）、SE-GEM（分段变体消除死神经元）；通过调节平滑度参数$N$和尺度参数$ε$适应不同网络架构。

Result: 在CIFAR-10+ResNet-56上，SE-GEM超越GELU（92.51% vs 92.44%）；GPT-2上GEM取得最低困惑度72.57（GELU为73.76）；BERT-small上E-GEM达到最佳验证损失6.656；发现$N=1$适用于深层CNN，$N=2$适用于Transformer的架构偏好规律。

Conclusion: GEM系列激活函数成功实现了类ReLU性能与纯有理算术的结合，首次在多个基准上超越GELU，揭示了不同网络架构对平滑度和尺度参数的差异化需求。

Abstract: The choice of activation function plays a crucial role in the optimization and performance of deep neural networks. While the Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) remains the dominant choice due to its simplicity and effectiveness, its lack of smoothness may hinder gradient-based optimization in deep architectures. In this work we propose a family of $C^{2N}$-smooth activation functions whose gate follows a log-logistic CDF, achieving ReLU-like performance with purely rational arithmetic. We introduce three variants: GEM (the base family), E-GEM (an $ε$-parameterized generalization enabling arbitrary $L^p$-approximation of ReLU), and SE-GEM (a piecewise variant eliminating dead neurons with $C^{2N}$ junction smoothness). An $N$-ablation study establishes $N=1$ as optimal for standard-depth networks, reducing the GELU deficit on CIFAR-100 + ResNet-56 from 6.10% to 2.12%. The smoothness parameter $N$ further reveals a CNN-transformer tradeoff: $N=1$ is preferred for deep CNNs, while $N=2$ is preferred for transformers. On MNIST, E-GEM ties the best baseline (99.23%). On CIFAR-10 + ResNet-56, SE-GEM ($ε=10^{-4}$) surpasses GELU (92.51% vs 92.44%) -- the first GEM-family activation to outperform GELU. On CIFAR-100 + ResNet-56, E-GEM reduces the GELU deficit from 6.10% (GEM $N=2$) to just 0.62%. On GPT-2 (124M), GEM achieves the lowest perplexity (72.57 vs 73.76 for GELU), with GEM $N=1$ also beating GELU (73.32). On BERT-small, E-GEM ($ε=10$) achieves the best validation loss (6.656) across all activations. The $ε$-parameterization reveals a scale-dependent optimum: small $ε$ ($10^{-4}$--$10^{-6}$) for deep CNNs and larger transformers, with the special case of small transformers (BERT-small) benefiting from large $ε$ ($ε=10$) due to its limited depth and unconstrained gradients.

</details>


### [67] [Evaluating Post-hoc Explanations of the Transformer-based Genome Language Model DNABERT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21690)
*Isabel Kurth,Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将AttnLRP方法适配到最先进的基因组语言模型DNABERT-2上，证明了Transformer模型也能像CNN一样提供可靠的生物学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在基因组序列上的预测解释能够提供生物学洞察，但目前尚不清楚这种解释能力是否能从卷积神经网络（CNN）迁移到表达能力更强的Transformer基因组语言模型（gLM）上。

Method: 将AttnLRP（一种将分层相关性传播扩展到注意力机制的方法）适配应用于DNABERT-2模型，提出了从token到核苷酸层面的解释迁移策略，并使用多种指标在基因组数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，AttnLRP能够产生可靠的解释，与已知的生物学模式相对应。与基线CNN的广泛比较显示，gLMs也能像CNN一样帮助获得生物学洞察。

Conclusion: 这项工作提升了gLMs的可解释性，解决了不同架构间相关性归因的可比性问题，证明了gLMs在生物学研究中的实用价值。

Abstract: Explaining deep neural network predictions on genome sequences enables biological insight and hypothesis generation-often of greater interest than predictive performance alone. While explanations of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have been shown to capture relevant patterns in genome sequences, it is unclear whether this transfers to more expressive Transformer-based genome language models (gLMs). To answer this question, we adapt AttnLRP, an extension of layer-wise relevance propagation to the attention mechanism, and apply it to the state-of-the-art gLM DNABERT-2. Thereby, we propose strategies to transfer explanations from token and nucleotide level. We evaluate the adaption of AttnLRP on genomic datasets using multiple metrics. Further, we provide an extensive comparison between the explanations of DNABERT-2 and a baseline CNN. Our results demonstrate that AttnLRP yields reliable explanations corresponding to known biological patterns. Hence, like CNNs, gLMs can also help derive biological insights. This work contributes to the explainability of gLMs and addresses the comparability of relevance attributions across different architectures.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Universal Tabular Embeddings: A Benchmark Across Data Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21696)
*Liane Vogel,Kavitha Srinivas,Niharika D'Souza,Sola Shirai,Oktie Hassanzadeh,Horst Samulowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TEmBed基准测试平台，用于系统评估表格嵌入模型在单元格、行、列和表格四个表示层级上的表现，发现最佳模型的选择取决于具体任务和表示层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管表格基础模型日益增多，但现有方法通常在特定任务设置下进行评估，难以直接比较，导致实践中哪种方法最有效尚不清晰。

Method: 引入TEmBed（表格嵌入测试床），一个综合基准测试平台，在单元格、行、列和表格四个表示层级上系统评估表格嵌入方法。

Result: 对多种表格表示学习模型进行评估后发现，不存在单一的通用最优模型，最佳模型的选择取决于具体任务和所需的表示层级。

Conclusion: 研究结果为现实应用中表格嵌入的选择提供了实践指导，并为开发更通用的表格表示模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models aim to learn universal representations of tabular data that transfer across tasks and domains, enabling applications such as table retrieval, semantic search and table-based prediction. Despite the growing number of such models, it remains unclear which approach works best in practice, as existing methods are often evaluated under task-specific settings that make direct comparison difficult. To address this, we introduce TEmBed, the Tabular Embedding Test Bed, a comprehensive benchmark for systematically evaluating tabular embeddings across four representation levels: cell, row, column, and table. Evaluating a diverse set of tabular representation learning models, we show that which model to use depends on the task and representation level. Our results offer practical guidance for selecting tabular embeddings in real-world applications and lay the groundwork for developing more general-purpose tabular representation models.

</details>


### [69] [Fairness under uncertainty in sequential decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21711)
*Michelle Seng Ah Lee,Kirtan Padh,David Watson,Niki Kilbertus,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了序贯决策中的不确定性分类法（模型、反馈和预测不确定性），揭示了不确定性在群体间不均匀分布如何导致不公平，并展示了通过不确定性感知的探索策略可以在保持机构目标的同时减少弱势群体的结果差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平机器学习研究主要集中在监督学习场景，但现实中的许多ML应用是在线序贯决策系统，先前决策会影响后续决策。由于未观测的反事实和有限样本，决策在不确定性下进行，这对代表性不足的群体造成严重后果——由于历史排斥和选择性反馈，这些群体系统性地被观测不足。例如，银行无法知道被拒绝的贷款是否会被偿还，且对边缘化群体的数据较少。

Method: 1. 引入序贯决策中的不确定性分类法：模型不确定性、反馈不确定性和预测不确定性，为评估不确定性在群体间不均匀分布的系统提供共享词汇。2. 通过反事实逻辑和强化学习形式化模型和反馈不确定性。3. 分析忽略未观测空间的策略对决策者（未实现的收益/损失）和主体（复合排斥、准入减少）的伤害。4. 提供算法示例和基于模拟偏置数据的实验，展示不确定性感知探索如何影响公平性指标。

Result: 1. 揭示了不平等的不确定性和选择性反馈如何产生差异。2. 证明可以通过不确定性感知的探索策略减少弱势群体的结果方差，同时保持机构目标（如期望效用）。3. 实验表明考虑不确定性可以改变公平性度量结果，为实践者提供诊断、审计和治理公平性风险的框架。

Conclusion: 当不确定性驱动不公平而非偶然噪声时，考虑不确定性对于公平有效的决策至关重要。该框架为实践者诊断、审计和治理公平性风险提供了工具，表明算法方法虽不能独自解决结构性不平等，但可以通过揭示歧视性偏见、阐明权衡和实现治理来支持社会技术决策系统。

Abstract: Fair machine learning (ML) methods help identify and mitigate the risk that algorithms encode or automate social injustices. Algorithmic approaches alone cannot resolve structural inequalities, but they can support socio-technical decision systems by surfacing discriminatory biases, clarifying trade-offs, and enabling governance. Although fairness is well studied in supervised learning, many real ML applications are online and sequential, with prior decisions informing future ones. Each decision is taken under uncertainty due to unobserved counterfactuals and finite samples, with dire consequences for under-represented groups, systematically under-observed due to historical exclusion and selective feedback. A bank cannot know whether a denied loan would have been repaid, and may have less data on marginalized populations.
  This paper introduces a taxonomy of uncertainty in sequential decision-making -- model, feedback, and prediction uncertainty -- providing shared vocabulary for assessing systems where uncertainty is unevenly distributed across groups. We formalize model and feedback uncertainty via counterfactual logic and reinforcement learning, and illustrate harms to decision makers (unrealized gains/losses) and subjects (compounding exclusion, reduced access) of policies that ignore the unobserved space. Algorithmic examples show it is possible to reduce outcome variance for disadvantaged groups while preserving institutional objectives (e.g. expected utility). Experiments on data simulated with varying bias show how unequal uncertainty and selective feedback produce disparities, and how uncertainty-aware exploration alters fairness metrics. The framework equips practitioners to diagnose, audit, and govern fairness risks. Where uncertainty drives unfairness rather than incidental noise, accounting for it is essential to fair and effective decision-making.

</details>


### [70] [Transferable Physics-Informed Representations via Closed-Form Head Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21761)
*Jian Cheng Wong,Isaac Yin Chung Lai,Pao-Hsiung Chiu,Chin Chun Ooi,Abhishek Gupta,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pi-PINN框架，通过在共享嵌入空间学习可迁移的物理信息表示，并利用伪逆进行闭式头部自适应，实现了对未知PDE实例的快速、准确求解，预测速度比传统PINN快100-1000倍，且仅需少量样本即可显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络（PINN）在缺乏训练样本时难以有效预测或求解新的偏微分方程（PDE），泛化能力不足。为了解决PINN在未知问题实例上泛化性能差的问题，提升其对不同PDE实例的求解效率和准确性。

Method: 提出了基于快速伪逆PINN框架（Pi-PINN）的可迁移学习方法。该方法在共享嵌入空间中学习可迁移的物理信息表示，并通过在PDE约束下使用最小二乘最优伪逆进行闭式头部自适应，从而快速求解已知和未知的PDE实例。此外，还研究了数据驱动的多任务学习损失与物理信息损失之间的协同作用。

Result: 在泊松方程、亥姆霍兹方程和伯格斯方程等多种PDE问题上验证了Pi-PINN的有效性。该方法在无需任何数据的情况下即可对未见实例进行预测，预测速度比典型PINN快100-1000倍；即使仅有两个训练样本，其预测的相对误差也比典型的数据驱动模型低10-100倍。

Conclusion: 利用可迁移表示结合闭式头部自适应的方法，能够显著提升PINN在PDE家族以及科学与工程应用中的效率和泛化能力，展示了其在解决未见PDE实例方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have garnered significant interest for their potential in solving partial differential equations (PDEs) that govern a wide range of physical phenomena. By incorporating physical laws into the learning process, PINN models have demonstrated the ability to learn physical outcomes reasonably well. However, current PINN approaches struggle to predict or solve new PDEs effectively when there is a lack of training examples, indicating they do not generalize well to unseen problem instances. In this paper, we present a transferable learning approach for PINNs premised on a fast Pseudoinverse PINN framework (Pi-PINN). Pi-PINN learns a transferable physics-informed representation in a shared embedding space and enables rapid solving of both known and unknown PDE instances via closed-form head adaptation using a least-squares-optimal pseudoinverse under PDE constraints. We further investigate the synergies between data-driven multi-task learning loss and physics-informed loss, providing insights into the design of more performant PINNs. We demonstrate the effectiveness of Pi-PINN on various PDE problems, including Poisson's equation, Helmholtz equation, and Burgers' equation, achieving fast and accurate physics-informed solutions without requiring any data for unseen instances. Pi-PINN can produce predictions 100-1000 times faster than a typical PINN, while producing predictions with 10-100 times lower relative error than a typical data-driven model even with only two training samples. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of transferable representations with closed-form head adaptation to enhance the efficiency and generalization of PINNs across PDE families and scientific and engineering applications.

</details>


### [71] [PrismaDV: Automated Task-Aware Data Unit Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21765)
*Hao Chen,Arnab Phani,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PrismaDV系统，通过分析下游任务代码生成任务感知的数据单元测试，并引入SIFTA框架优化测试生成，显著提升了数据错误的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化数据单元测试框架大多是任务无关的，在验证数据集时未考虑消费数据的下游代码的语义和需求，导致无法有效识别对下游应用有实际影响的数据错误。

Method: 提出PrismaDV复合AI系统，通过联合分析下游任务代码和数据集特征来识别数据访问模式、推断隐式数据假设并生成任务感知的可执行数据单元测试；同时提出SIFTA（选择性信息反馈任务适应）提示优化框架，利用测试执行和下游任务的稀缺结果来自动优化提示。

Result: 在涵盖五个数据集、60个任务的基准测试中，PrismaDV在生成能反映数据错误端到端影响的单元测试方面，持续优于任务无关和任务感知基线；使用SIFTA自动学习的提示优于手工编写或通用提示优化器生成的提示。

Conclusion: 通过将下游任务语义纳入数据验证流程并结合自动提示优化，PrismaDV为现代企业提供了一种更有效的数据质量保障方案，研究团队公开了基准测试和原型实现以促进相关研究。

Abstract: Data is a central resource for modern enterprises, and data validation is essential for ensuring the reliability of downstream applications. However, existing automated data unit testing frameworks are largely task-agnostic: they validate datasets without considering the semantics and requirements of the code that consumes the data.
  We present PrismaDV, a compound AI system that analyzes downstream task code together with dataset profiles to identify data access patterns, infer implicit data assumptions, and generate task-aware executable data unit tests. To further adapt the data unit tests over time to specific datasets and downstream tasks, we propose "Selective Informative Feedback for Task Adaptation" (SIFTA), a prompt-optimization framework that leverages the scarce outcomes from the execution of data unit tests and downstream tasks. We evaluate PrismaDV on two new benchmarks spanning 60 tasks across five datasets, where it consistently outperforms both task-agnostic and task-aware baselines in generating unit tests that reflect the end-to-end impact of data errors. Furthermore, we show that with SIFTA, we can automatically learn prompts for PrismaDV's modules that outperform prompts written by hand or generated from a generic prompt optimizer. We publicly release our benchmarks and prototype implementation.

</details>


### [72] [An effective variant of the Hartigan $k$-means algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21798)
*François Clément,Stefan Steinerberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出Hartigan算法的一个微小变体能够在已有改进基础上再提升2%-5%的聚类效果，且随着维度或k值的增加，改进幅度会更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-means聚类问题通常使用Lloyd算法，但Hartigan算法在几乎所有情况下都能产生更好的结果（典型改进5%-10%）。本文旨在探索是否能进一步改进Hartigan算法的性能。

Method: 提出了Hartigan方法的一个非常微小的变体，具体通过调整Hartigan算法的某些操作细节来实现改进。

Result: 该微小变体能够在Hartigan算法改进的基础上再获得2%-5%的性能提升；当维度或聚类数k增加时，改进效果会变得更加明显。

Conclusion: 即使是经典的聚类算法，通过细微的算法变体也能获得显著的性能提升，这为聚类算法的优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The k-means problem is perhaps the classical clustering problem and often synonymous with Lloyd's algorithm (1957). It has become clear that Hartigan's algorithm (1975) gives better results in almost all cases, Telgarsky-Vattani note a typical improvement of $5\%$ -- $10\%$. We point out that a very minor variation of Hartigan's method leads to another $2\%$ -- $5\%$ improvement; the improvement tends to become larger when either dimension or $k$ increase.

</details>


### [73] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在商空间上进行扩散建模的通用框架，并将其应用于分子结构生成。相比传统的群等变扩散模型，该方法避免了学习群作用对应的分量，简化了学习难度，且保证了采样器能正确恢复目标分布，在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上表现优于以往的对称性处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成式AI和科学领域（如分子3D结构生成）取得了巨大成功。由于特定任务内在的问题结构，系统往往存在对称性，即可以通过群作用将相互转换的对象视为等价，因此目标分布本质上定义在关于该群的商空间上。现有的启发式对齐策略缺乏合适的采样器，而传统的群等变扩散模型学习难度较大。

Method: 建立了一个在一般商空间上进行扩散建模的形式化框架。该框架将问题从原始空间转移到商空间，从而减少了学习群作用对应分量的必要性，简化了学习过程。研究者将该框架具体应用于遵循特殊欧几里得群SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成任务，并设计了能够保证恢复目标分布的采样器。

Result: 在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上的实验验证了该方法的论点。实验结果表明，基于原则的商空间扩散模型优于以往的对称性处理方法，提供了一个新的、更有效的框架。

Conclusion: 本文提出的商空间扩散模型框架为处理对称性问题提供了一种新的范式。它不仅在理论上保证了采样的正确性，还简化了模型的学习过程，在分子结构生成等科学任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [74] [GFlowState: Visualizing the Training of Generative Flow Networks Beyond the Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21830)
*Florian Holeczek,Andreas Hinterreiter,Alex Hernandez-Garcia,Marc Streit,Christina Humer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GFlowState，一个用于可视化和解析生成流网络(GFlowNets)训练过程的可视分析系统，通过多种视图揭示模型采样行为和训练动态，帮助开发者调试和评估模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNets虽然在分子和材料发现等领域表现出强大能力，但其训练动态难以解释。现有的标准机器学习工具仅能进行指标跟踪，无法揭示模型如何探索样本空间、构建采样轨迹或在训练过程中转移采样概率，缺乏对采样行为和策略演化的深入分析手段。

Method: 提出了GFlowState可视分析系统，引入了多个可视化视图：候选排名图表、状态投影、轨迹网络的节点链接图以及转移热图。这些可视化组件使用户能够分析采样轨迹、对比样本空间与参考数据集的关系，并深入分析训练动态。

Result: 通过案例研究证明，该系统能够支持跨应用领域的GFlowNets调试和质量评估。系统能够帮助用户调查采样行为和策略演化，识别探索不足的区域和训练失败的根源。

Conclusion: 通过使GFlowNets的结构动态变得可观察，本文工作增强了其可解释性，能够在实践中加速GFlowNet的开发进程，为GFlowNet开发者和用户提供了有效的分析工具。

Abstract: We present GFlowState, a visual analytics system designed to illuminate the training process of Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets or GFNs). GFlowNets are a probabilistic framework for generating samples proportionally to a reward function. While GFlowNets have proved to be powerful tools in applications such as molecule and material discovery, their training dynamics remain difficult to interpret. Standard machine learning tools allow metric tracking but do not reveal how models explore the sample space, construct sample trajectories, or shift sampling probabilities during training. Our solution, GFlowState, allows users to analyze sampling trajectories, compare the sample space relative to reference datasets, and analyze the training dynamics. To this end, we introduce multiple views, including a chart of candidate rankings, a state projection, a node-link diagram of the trajectory network, and a transition heatmap. These visualizations enable GFlowNet developers and users to investigate sampling behavior and policy evolution, and to identify underexplored regions and sources of training failure. Case studies demonstrate how the system supports debugging and assessing the quality of GFlowNets across application domains. By making the structural dynamics of GFlowNets observable, our work enhances their interpretability and can accelerate GFlowNet development in practice.

</details>


### [75] [A Scale-Adaptive Framework for Joint Spatiotemporal Super-Resolution with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21903)
*Max Defez,Filippo Quarenghi,Mathieu Vrac,Stephan Mandt,Tom Beucler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种尺度自适应的视频超分辨率框架，通过分解为条件均值预测和残差条件扩散模型，实现了同一架构在不同时空放大因子下的复用，并在法国降水再分析数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习视频超分辨率方法通常只针对空间或时间单一维度进行超分辨率重建，且联合时空模型往往局限于特定的超分辨率因子组合，这限制了模型在不同空间分辨率和时间频率之间的迁移能力。因此需要一种能够适应多种超分辨率因子且保持性能的统一框架。

Method: 提出尺度自适应框架，将时空超分辨率分解为两个部分：1）使用注意力机制的条件均值确定性预测；2）残差条件扩散模型，并可选地引入质量守恒变换以保持降水总量的守恒。通过重新调整三个与因子相关的超参数实现尺度自适应：扩散噪声调度幅度beta（较大因子使用更大值以增加多样性）、时间上下文长度L（设定为在不同时间频率下保持相当的注意力范围）以及可选的质量守恒函数f（针对大因子进行约束以限制极端值的放大）。该方法假设较大的超分辨率因子主要增加欠定性而非改变条件均值结构。

Result: 在法国降水再分析数据集（Comephore）上的实验表明，同一架构可以覆盖空间超分辨率因子1到25、时间超分辨率因子1到6的范围，成功实现了跨尺度的可复用架构和调参方案。模型能够保持质量守恒，同时生成高质量的超分辨率降水场。

Conclusion: 该框架为联合时空超分辨率提供了一种跨尺度可复用的解决方案，通过简单的超参数调整即可适应不同的放大因子，避免了为每个特定因子组合重新设计或训练全新模型的需要，为气候应用中的多尺度超分辨率问题提供了实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep-learning video super-resolution has progressed rapidly, but climate applications typically super-resolve (increase resolution) either space or time, and joint spatiotemporal models are often designed for a single pair of super-resolution (SR) factors (upscaling spatial and temporal ratio between the low-resolution sequence and the high-resolution sequence), limiting transfer across spatial resolutions and temporal cadences (frame rates). We present a scale-adaptive framework that reuses the same architecture across factors by decomposing spatiotemporal SR into a deterministic prediction of the conditional mean, with attention, and a residual conditional diffusion model, with an optional mass-conservation (same precipitation amount in inputs and outputs) transform to preserve aggregated totals. Assuming that larger SR factors primarily increase underdetermination (hence required context and residual uncertainty) rather than changing the conditional-mean structure, scale adaptivity is achieved by retuning three factor-dependent hyperparameters before retraining: the diffusion noise schedule amplitude beta (larger for larger factors to increase diversity), the temporal context length L (set to maintain comparable attention horizons across cadences) and optionally a third, the mass-conservation function f (tapered to limit the amplification of extremes for large factors). Demonstrated on reanalysis precipitation over France (Comephore), the same architecture spans super-resolution factors from 1 to 25 in space and 1 to 6 in time, yielding a reusable architecture and tuning recipe for joint spatiotemporal super-resolution across scales.

</details>


### [76] [Low-Rank Adaptation Redux for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21905)
*Bingcong Li,Yilang Zhang,Georgios B. Giannakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信号处理视角系统综述了LoRA技术，将现代适配器设计与经典低秩建模工具相联系，从架构设计、高效优化和相关应用三个维度分析了LoRA的底层机制与原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA已成为基础模型参数高效微调的事实标准并衍生出众多变体，但目前仍缺乏明确的架构选择、优化技术和部署约束指导原则，亟需建立系统性的理论框架来理解其有效性机制。

Method: 采用信号处理(SP)视角重新审视LoRA，将适配器设计与经典的SVD分解、低秩建模及逆问题求解等SP原理相联系，从架构设计（SVD分解、秩增强、跨层张量化）、高效优化（初始化、交替求解器、规范不变优化、参数感知方法）和应用场景三个互补维度进行技术机制分析。

Result: 系统梳理了LoRA的技术体系：在架构层面建立了基于SVD分解、秩增强构造和跨层张量化的设计原则；在优化层面阐明了初始化策略、交替求解器、规范不变优化等方法的理论基础；在应用层面拓展了LoRA在大模型全生命周期（预训练、后训练、服务部署）中的适用场景。

Conclusion: 信号处理与深度学习的融合为PEFT方法提供了有原则的设计词汇和理论框架，同时深度学习在大规模和开销方面的独特挑战也为SP社区开辟了新的研究方向，两者形成了双向促进的研究前沿。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as the de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of foundation models, enabling the adaptation of billion-parameter networks with minimal computational and memory overhead. Despite its empirical success and rapid proliferation of variants, it remains elusive which architectural choices, optimization techniques, and deployment constraints should guide practical method selection. This overview revisits LoRA through the lens of signal processing (SP), bridging modern adapter designs with classical low-rank modeling tools and inverse problems, as well as highlighting how SP principles can inform principled advances of fine-tuning approaches. Rather than providing a comprehensive enumeration and empirical comparisons of LoRA variants, emphasis is placed on the technical mechanisms underpinning these approaches to justify their effectiveness. These advances are categorized into three complementary axes: architectural design, efficient optimization, and pertinent applications. The first axis builds on singular value decomposition (SVD)-based factorization, rank-augmentation constructions, and cross-layer tensorization, while the second axis deals with initialization, alternating solvers, gauge-invariant optimization, and parameterization-aware methods. Beyond fine-tuning, emerging applications of LoRA are accounted across the entire lifecycle of large models, ranging from pre- and post-training to serving/deployment. Finally, open research directions are outlined at the confluence of SP and deep learning to catalyze a bidirectional frontier: classical SP tools provide a principled vocabulary for designing principled PEFT methods, while the unique challenges facing modern deep learning, especially the overwhelming scale and prohibitive overhead, also offer new research lines benefiting the SP community in return.

</details>


### [77] [The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21923)
*Natalie Collina,Jiuyao Lu,Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文确定了批量设置下多校准的最优样本复杂度为$\widetilde\Theta(\varepsilon^{-3})$（当$|G|\le\varepsilon^{-\kappa},\kappa>0$时），并在$\kappa=0$处发现急剧阈值现象（复杂度降至$\widetilde\Theta(\varepsilon^{-2})$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究批量设置下多校准的极小极大样本复杂度，以理解其与边缘校准的样本复杂度差异，并揭示多校准在批量和在线设置中的难度关系。

Method: 通过online-to-batch转换构造随机预测器实现上界；建立下界模板，将下界推广至可引出性质的正则类，并结合Hu et al. (2025)的在线上界。

Result: 对于$|G|\le\varepsilon^{-\kappa}$且$\kappa>0$的情形，多校准样本复杂度为$\widetilde\Theta(\varepsilon^{-3})$；当$\kappa=0$时急剧降至$\widetilde\Theta(\varepsilon^{-2})$。加权$L_p$多校准度量的最优指数为$3/p$（$1\le p\le 2$）。对expectiles和有界密度分位数等可引出性质也给出了匹配的上下界。

Conclusion: 多校准的样本复杂度严格高于边缘校准（$\widetilde\Theta(\varepsilon^{-2})$）；多校准在批量和在线设置中难度相当，而边缘校准在在线设置中更难；在$\kappa=0$处存在急剧阈值现象。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon.
  More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.

</details>


### [78] [Fine-Tuning Regimes Define Distinct Continual Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21927)
*Paul-Tiberiu Iordache,Elena Burceanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续学习（CL）方法评估中常被忽视的关键变量——微调机制（即可训练参数子空间）。研究发现，在不同可训练深度机制下，四种标准CL方法的相对排名并不一致，且更深层的适应机制伴随着更大的更新幅度和更高的遗忘率。结论表明CL中的比较性结论强烈依赖于所选的微调机制，建议将可训练深度作为明确的实验因素纳入评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续学习基准测试通常将微调机制（fine-tuning regime）视为固定条件，而作者认为由可训练参数子空间定义的微调机制本身应作为一个关键评估变量。作者旨在探究改变可训练深度如何影响当前任务拟合和知识保持的有效更新信号，以及这种变化是否会导致方法比较结果的改变。

Method: 作者将适应机制形式化为固定可训练子空间上的投影优化，理论分析表明改变可训练深度会改变有效更新信号。在实验设计上，作者在任务增量学习设置下，测试了五种可训练深度机制和四种标准方法（online EWC、LwF、SI和GEM），并在MNIST、Fashion MNIST、KMNIST、QMNIST和CIFAR-100五个基准数据集上，每个数据集测试了11种任务顺序。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）方法的相对排名在不同机制间未能保持一致；2）更深层的适应机制与更大的参数更新幅度、更高的遗忘率以及两者之间更强的相关性相关联。这些发现验证了方法比较不必跨机制保持不变的假设。

Conclusion: 持续学习中的比较性结论强烈依赖于所选择的微调机制。因此，作者建议采用机制感知的评估协议，将可训练深度作为明确的实验因素进行处理，而非固定不变的条件，以确保持续学习方法评估的全面性和可靠性。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) studies how models acquire tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned knowledge. Despite substantial progress in benchmarking CL methods, comparative evaluations typically keep the fine-tuning regime fixed. In this paper, we argue that the fine-tuning regime, defined by the trainable parameter subspace, is itself a key evaluation variable. We formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable subspaces, showing that changing the trainable depth alters the effective update signal through which both current task fitting and knowledge preservation operate. This analysis motivates the hypothesis that method comparisons need not be invariant across regimes. We test this hypothesis in task incremental CL, five trainable depth regimes, and four standard methods: online EWC, LwF, SI, and GEM. Across five benchmark datasets, namely MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, and CIFAR-100, and across 11 task orders per dataset, we find that the relative ranking of methods is not consistently preserved across regimes. We further show that deeper adaptation regimes are associated with larger update magnitudes, higher forgetting, and a stronger relationship between the two. These results show that comparative conclusions in CL can depend strongly on the chosen fine-tuning regime, motivating regime-aware evaluation protocols that treat trainable depth as an explicit experimental factor.

</details>


### [79] [Temporal Taskification in Streaming Continual Learning: A Source of Evaluation Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21930)
*Nicolae Filat,Ahmed Hussain,Konstantinos Kalogiannis,Elena Burceanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了流式持续学习中的“时序任务化”（将连续数据流分割为离散任务）这一预处理步骤会显著影响评估结果，提出了一套分析框架来量化这种影响，并证明不同的分割方式会导致不同的基准结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式持续学习研究通常将连续数据流通过时序分区转化为离散的任务序列。作者认为这一“时序任务化”步骤并非中立的数据预处理，而是评估体系的一个结构性组成部分：对于同一数据流，不同的有效分割方式可能诱发不同的持续学习机制（如可塑性-稳定性权衡），从而导致不同的基准测试结论。目前缺乏对该效应的系统性研究。

Method: 1. 提出了一个任务化级别的分析框架，基于可塑性与稳定性分布特征。
2. 定义了分布间的“距离”来量化不同任务化方式的差异。
3. 引入了边界-分布敏感性指标，用于在模型训练前诊断微小的边界扰动如何改变诱发机制。
4. 在CESNET-Timeseries24网络流量预测任务上，固定数据流、模型和训练预算，仅改变时序任务化方式（分别采用9天、30天和44天分割），评估持续微调、经验回放、弹性权重巩固和Learning without Forgetting四种方法。

Result: 1. 在不同的分割方式下，预测误差、遗忘程度和后向迁移等指标均发生显著变化，证明任务化方式本身能实质性影响持续学习评估结果。
2. 较短的任务分割（如9天）会导致分布层面的模式更加嘈杂、结构距离更大、BPS更高，表明对边界扰动更加敏感。
3. 结论表明，流式持续学习的基准测试结果不仅取决于学习器和数据流本身，还取决于数据流如何被任务化。

Conclusion: 时序任务化应被视为一个“一等评估变量”，在设计和报告流式持续学习基准测试时需要明确指定并审慎选择，因为它直接影响基准测试的结论和模型性能评估。

Abstract: Streaming Continual Learning (CL) typically converts a continuous stream into a sequence of discrete tasks through temporal partitioning. We argue that this temporal taskification step is not a neutral preprocessing choice, but a structural component of evaluation: different valid splits of the same stream can induce different CL regimes and therefore different benchmark conclusions. To study this effect, we introduce a taskification-level framework based on plasticity and stability profiles, a profile distance between taskifications, and Boundary-Profile Sensitivity (BPS), which diagnoses how strongly small boundary perturbations alter the induced regime before any CL model is trained. We evaluate continual finetuning, Experience Replay, Elastic Weight Consolidation, and Learning without Forgetting on network traffic forecasting with CESNET-Timeseries24, keeping the stream, model, and training budget fixed while varying only the temporal taskification. Across 9-, 30-, and 44-day splits, we observe substantial changes in forecasting error, forgetting, and backward transfer, showing that taskification alone can materially affect CL evaluation. We further find that shorter taskifications induce noisier distribution-level patterns, larger structural distances, and higher BPS, indicating greater sensitivity to boundary perturbations. These results show that benchmark conclusions in streaming CL depend not only on the learner and the data stream, but also on how that stream is taskified, motivating temporal taskification as a first-class evaluation variable.

</details>

### [80] [StarLoc: Pinpointing Transmitting LEO Satellites from a Single Passive Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21147)
*Ishani Janveja,Jida Zhang,Emerson Sie,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出StarLoc系统，利用轨道建模和干涉测角技术，仅用三个平面天线即可实现对LEO卫星的三维定位，在Starlink卫星上验证角度误差0.7度、距离误差5公里。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星的三维定位对于频谱管理、轨道确定及GPS失效备份等应用至关重要，现有方法存在挑战，亟需一种高效的定位方案。

Method: 提出StarLoc系统，结合轨道动力学建模与新型干涉式三维到达角估计技术；利用卫星运动受轨道动力学约束、在三维空间沿二维流形运动的特性，减少自由度，仅需部署在二维平面内的三个天线即可实现三维定位与跟踪。

Result: 在81颗Starlink卫星的真实信号传输上评估，StarLoc对卫星的三维到达角估计误差在0.7度以内，轨道距离估计误差在5公里以内。

Conclusion: StarLoc证明了利用轨道动力学约束可以有效简化LEO卫星三维定位所需的硬件配置，以较低成本实现了高精度的空间目标定位与跟踪，为相关应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on 3D localization of transmitting satellites in low Earth orbits (LEO). 3D localization of transmitters in low orbits is an important emerging problem for many applications such as spectrum management, orbit determination, and backup for GPS failures in orbit. We present StarLoc -- a system to geolocate transmitters in space using a combination of orbital modeling and a new interferometric 3D angle-of-arrival estimation technique. StarLoc's design relies on a unique insight -- the motion of satellites is governed by orbital dynamics and is therefore along a 2D manifold in a 3D space. This reduces the degrees of freedom in satellite motion and allows us to 3D-locate and track a satellite with just three antennas in a 2D plane. We evaluate the system using signal transmissions from 81 Starlink satellites. Our results show that StarLoc can estimate the 3D-angle of a satellite within 0.7 degrees and the orbital range within 5 km. Our dataset and implementation are available at: https://connectedsystemslab.github.io/starloc.

</details>


### [81] [SPAC: Automating FPGA-based Network Switches with Protocol Adaptive Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21881)
*Guoyu Li,Yang Cao,Lucas H L Ng,Alexander Charlton,Qianzhou Wang,Will Punter,Philippos Papaphilippou,Ce Guo,Hongxiang Fan,Wayne Luk,Saman Amarasinghe,Ajay Brahmakshatriya*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPAC框架，通过自动化生成针对特定协议和流量模式优化的FPGA网络交换机，在降低资源消耗的同时显著减少延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着新兴应用的网络需求分化，延迟敏感型服务需要最小逻辑延迟，而超大规模训练和集合通信需要持续的线速吞吐量。这种分化迫切需要针对专用协议和应用特定流量模式定制的网络交换机。

Method: SPAC采用统一工作流程，包括：1）用于协议-架构协同设计的领域特定语言（DSL）；2）基于HLS的模块化自适应交换组件库；3）流量感知的设计空间探索（DSE）引擎；4）多保真度仿真栈，用于在部署前快速识别帕累托最优设计。

Result: SPAC生成的设计相比传统方法减少LUT和BRAM使用量分别达55%和53%；与固定架构方案相比，在各任务中实现7.8%至38.4%的延迟降低，同时保持合理的资源消耗和丢包率。

Conclusion: 通过针对特定工作负载定制微架构和协议，SPAC成功实现了跨延迟敏感传感器网络、高频交易网络到超大规模数据中心交换架构等多个真实场景的有效适配，证明了领域特定自适应定制的优越性。

Abstract: With network requirements diverging across emerging applications, latency-critical services demand minimal logic delay, while hyperscale training and collectives require sustained line-rate throughput for synchronized bulk transfers. This divergence creates an urgent need for custom network switches tailored to specialized protocols and application-specific traffic patterns. This paper presents SPAC (Switch and Protocol Adaptive Customization), a novel approach that automates the generation of FPGA-based network switches co-optimized for custom protocols and application-specific traffic patterns. SPAC introduces a unified workflow with a domain-specific language (DSL) for protocol-architecture co-design, a library of modular HLS-based adaptive switch components, and a trace-aware Design Space Exploration (DSE) engine. By providing a multi-fidelity simulation stack, SPAC enables rapid identification of Pareto-optimal designs prior to deployment. We demonstrate the efficacy of the domain-specific adaptation of SPAC across a spectrum of real-world scenarios, spanning from latency-sensitive sensor and HFT networks to hyperscale datacenter fabrics. Experimental results show that by tailoring the micro-architecture and protocol to the specific workload, SPAC-generated designs reduce LUT and BRAM usage by 55% and 53%, respectively. Compared to fixed-architecture counterparts, SPAC delivers latency reductions ranging from 7.8% to 38.4% across various tasks while maintaining adequate resource consumption and packet drop rate.

</details>


### [82] [SparKV: Overhead-Aware KV Cache Loading for Efficient On-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21231)
*Hongyao Liu,Liuqun Zhai,Junyi Wang,Zhengru Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SparKV框架，通过自适应KV加载策略结合云端流式传输与本地计算，有效降低了设备端大模型推理的延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端大语言模型推理面临硬件资源受限和预填充阶段成本高昂的双重挑战，该阶段需处理完整输入上下文以构建KV缓存，成为主要性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出SparKV自适应KV加载框架，建模单个KV块的成本并决策其应从云端流式传输还是本地计算，通过重叠两条执行路径降低延迟。针对无线连接波动和边缘资源变化，在运行时优化离线生成的调度计划以平衡通信与计算成本。

Result: 在多种数据集、LLM和边缘设备上的实验表明，SparKV将首个Token时间（TTFT）减少1.3x-5.1x，响应质量影响可忽略，同时将单次请求能耗降低1.5x-3.3x。

Conclusion: SparKV框架证明了其在真实设备端部署场景中的鲁棒性和实用性，为资源受限环境下的高效LLM推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient inference for on-device Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging due to limited hardware resources and the high cost of the prefill stage, which processes the full input context to construct Key-Value (KV) caches. We present SparKV, an adaptive KV loading framework that combines cloud-based KV streaming with on-device computation. SparKV models the cost of individual KV chunks and decides whether each chunk should be streamed or computed locally, while overlapping the two execution paths to reduce latency. To handle fluctuations in wireless connectivity and edge resource availability, SparKV further refines offline-generated schedules at runtime to rebalance communication and computation costs. Experiments across diverse datasets, LLMs, and edge devices show that SparKV reduces Time-to-First-Token by 1.3$x-5.1x with negligible impact on response quality, while lowering per-request energy consumption by 1.5x to 3.3x, demonstrating its robustness and practicality for real-world on-device deployment.

</details>


### [83] [An Efficient Wireless iBCI Headstage with Adaptive ADC Sample Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21247)
*Hongyao Liu,Junyi Wang,Liuqun Zhai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种服务端驱动的无线植入式脑机接口头阶段设计，通过自适应ADC采样和锋电位检测实现源级效率优化，功耗降低高达40 mW，FPGA资源占用减少3.2倍，同时保持或提高解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线植入式脑机接口（iBCI）在临床和日常应用中日益重要，但面临严重的功耗和数据吞吐量限制。传统应用层压缩方法计算密集且效率有限，需要一种更高效的解决方案来缓解这些瓶颈。

Method: 采用服务端驱动架构实现源级效率优化，与传统应用层压缩不同。服务器学习最优的电极特定采样率向量，动态重配置ADC硬件。该策略直接在采集层（ADC和放大器）减少数据量，而非依赖计算密集的数字化后处理。开发了32通道无线BCI头阶段原型并开源。

Result: 在多个受试者和阵列上的广泛实验表明：功耗降低高达40 mW，FPGA资源利用率减少3.2倍，同时在运动和视觉任务中保持或超过解码精度。

Conclusion: 该设计为长期体内记录提供了高度实用的解决方案，有效解决了无线iBCI的功耗和吞吐量瓶颈问题，且开源原型便于推广应用。

Abstract: Implantable Brain-Computer Interfaces (iBCIs) are increasingly pivotal in clinical and daily applications. However, wireless iBCIs face severe constraints in power consumption and data throughput. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose a wireless iBCI headstage featuring adaptive ADC sampling and spike detection. Distinguishing our design from traditional application-layer compression, we employ a server-driven architecture that achieves source-level efficiency. Specifically, the server learns an optimal, electrode-specific sample rate vector to dynamically reconfigure the ADC hardware. This strategy reduces data volume directly at the acquisition layer (ADC and amplifier) rather than relying on computationally intensive post-digitization processing. Extensive experiments across diverse subjects and arrays demonstrate a power reduction of up to 40 mW and a 3.2$\times$ decrease in FPGA resource utilization, all while maintaining or exceeding decoding accuracy in both motor and visual tasks. This design offers a highly practical solution for long-term in-vivo recording.Our prototype is open-sourced in: https://github.com/liuhongyao99cs/32-Channel-Wireless-BCI-Headstage.

</details>


### [84] [Iterative Receiver Processing at Relays in PNC-Enabled Multi-Hop Underwater Acoustic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21819)
*Gewei Zhang,Deqing Wang,Lizhao You,Xiangming Cai,Liqun Fu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对PNC enabled多跳UWA网络，提出了一种基于OFDM的迭代检测译码接收机设计，通过自适应因子图检测和软信息优化等算法，有效解决了多跳网络中的误码累积问题，实验验证了其在实际水声信道中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理层网络编码（PNC）虽能提升双向多跳水声（UWA）网络的端到端吞吐量，但UWA信道的多径时延扩展和多普勒引起的载波间干扰（ICI）会降低PNC传输的可靠性。更关键的是，在多跳网络中，跨多个中继节点的误码累积会导致端到端误码率（BER）显著增加。

Method: 提出了基于OFDM调制的迭代检测与译码处理策略，包含三个核心算法：（i）适用于时变UWA信道的自适应信道感知因子图检测算法；（ii）提高译码器到检测器的信息反馈精度的奇偶校验约束软信息优化算法；（iii）基于叠加模型的线性最小均方误差（LMMSE）检测算法，作为低计算复杂度的替代方案。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在相对速度1.5 m/s的UWA信道和8 dB信噪比下，自适应检测算法可实现10^-5量级的误码率。台湾海峡的湖上实验和海试均证实，所提迭代接收机算法在实际UWA信道条件下BER性能优于基线方案，证明了其在实际多跳部署中的鲁棒性和适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代接收机算法有效解决了PNC enabled多跳UWA网络中的误码累积问题，在实际水声信道条件下展现出优异的误码性能和鲁棒性，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Physical-layer network coding (PNC) can increase end-to-end throughput in bi-directional multi-hop underwater acoustic (UWA) networks. However, multipath delay spread and Doppler-induced inter-carrier interference (ICI) in UWA channels can degrade the reliability of PNC transmission in a three-node relay configuration. More critically, error accumulation across multiple relay nodes leads to a pronounced increase in the end-to-end bit error rate (BER) in multi-hop networks. To address this issue, we develop an iterative detection and decoding processing strategy for relay nodes within a PNC-enabled multi-hop UWA network based on orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) modulation. The proposed design integrates three key algorithms: (i) an adaptive channel-aware factor graph detection algorithm that is suited for time-varying UWA channels; (ii) a parity-check-constrained soft-information refinement algorithm that improves the accuracy of the information feedback from the decoder to the detector; and (iii) a linear minimum mean square error (LMMSE) detection algorithm based on a superimposed model, which offers low computational complexity as an alternative scheme. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the adaptive detection algorithm achieves BERs on the order of $10^{-5}$ at a relative velocity of 1.5 m/s UWA channel and a signal-to-noise (SNR) of 8~dB. Both lake experiments and sea trials in the Taiwan Strait confirm that the proposed iterative receiver algorithms outperform baseline schemes in terms of BER performance under practical UWA channel conditions, showing their robustness and applicability in real multi-hop deployments.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [AGNT2: Autonomous Agent Economies on Interaction-Optimized Layer 2 Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21129)
*Anbang Ruan,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AGNT2是一个专为AI代理和微服务链上协调设计的三层执行架构，通过Sidecar部署模式和分层设计解决现有区块链对高频服务调用的不适配问题，目标TPS达百万级但受限于数据可用性带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链Layer 2方案（如Optimism、Arbitrum、zkSync）针对人类发起的金融交易优化，而自主AI代理产生的是高频、语义丰富的服务调用。现有链将这些交互视为通用调用数据，迫使身份、托管、依赖排序和会话状态在执行层之上编码，成本效益低下。

Method: AGNT2采用三层堆栈：(1) Sidecar部署模式将任何Docker容器转变为链上代理，无需修改应用代码；(2) Layer Top P2P状态通道用于已建立的双边对（<100ms，每对目标1K-5K TPS），Layer Core依赖感知排序Rollup用于首次接触和多方交互（500ms-2s，目标300K-500K TPS），Layer Root结算层使用计算欺诈证明锚定到任意EVM L1；(3) 代理原生执行环境将服务调用、身份、声誉、能力和会话上下文作为一级协议对象。

Result: 仿真和分析建模支持该架构设计，原型测量验证了选定组件。目前实际部署受DA吞吐量限制约为10K-100K TPS，与目标上限存在约100倍差距。尚未实现端到端的Layer Core完整实现。

Conclusion: 代理经济需要专用的执行层，而非将通用链重新用于代理场景。AGNT2论证了执行层系统问题的解决方案，但数据可用性带宽差距仍是制约性能的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Current blockchain Layer 2 solutions, including Optimism, Arbitrum, zkSync, and their derivatives, optimize for human-initiated financial transactions. Autonomous AI agents instead generate high-frequency, semantically rich service invocations among mutually untrusting principals. Existing chains treat those interactions as generic calldata, forcing identity, escrow, dependency ordering, and session state to be encoded above the execution layer at the wrong cost point. We present AGNT2, a three-tier stack purpose-built for agent and microservice coordination on-chain. AGNT2 combines: (1) a sidecar deployment pattern that turns any Docker container into an on-chain agent without application-code modification; (2) Layer Top P2P state channels for established bilateral pairs (<100 ms, rough design target 1K-5K TPS per pair, 10M+ aggregate TPS design envelope under endpoint-resource limits), Layer Core as a dependency-aware sequenced rollup for first-contact and multi-party interactions (500 ms-2 s, 300K-500K TPS design target), and Layer Root settlement with computational fraud proofs anchored to any EVM L1; and (3) an agent-native execution environment plus interaction trie that make service invocation, identity, reputation, capabilities, and session context first-class protocol objects. This paper focuses on the execution-layer systems problem: sequencing, state, settlement, and the data-availability (DA) bandwidth gap that bounds all three. Simulation and analytical modeling support the architecture, and prototype measurements validate selected components, but no end-to-end Layer Core implementation exists yet. Practical deployment is currently constrained to roughly 10K-100K TPS by DA throughput, leaving a ~100x gap at the target ceiling. AGNT2 argues that the agent economy requires a dedicated execution layer rather than a general-purpose chain repurposed for agents.

</details>


### [86] [Role of diversity in team performance: the case of missing expertise, an agent based simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21328)
*Tamás Kiss*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于主体的模型来研究隐藏分布的影响以及功能多样性构成对团队沟通和绩效的影响。模拟结果表明，人际功能多样性（IFD）和主导功能多样性（DFD）对绩效和沟通的影响取决于情境因素，可能产生促进或抑制作用。研究还发现需要引入第三个度量指标来捕捉团队的总体专业知识，以全面解释实证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典实验研究在考察社会系统时通常只分析测量变量分布的前一两个模态，在分析情境效应方面存在困难。功能性多样性研究中的管理团队描述包含个体管理者和团队能力的复杂分布，需要研究隐藏分布的影响以及功能性多样性构成对团队沟通和绩效的影响。

Method: 开发了一个基于主体的模型，并进行了一系列模拟实验。通过模拟不同的情境条件（如互动主体间的沟通方案、功能构成），研究人际功能多样性（IFD）和主导功能多样性（DFD）对团队绩效和沟通的影响。

Result: 模拟结果表明，IFD和DFD对绩效和沟通的影响取决于情境因素（如沟通方案、功能构成），可能产生促进或抑制作用。模拟结果还显示，除了IFD和DFD之外，需要第三个度量指标来捕捉团队的总体专业知识，以全面解释实证研究发现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了功能性多样性对团队绩效和沟通的情境依赖性影响，强调了在解释实证发现时需要考虑更全面的测量指标。这为管理团队研究提供了新的理论视角和方法论工具。

Abstract: Theory and empirical research on management teams' influence on firm performance have witnessed continuous development, and by now incorporate numerous details. Classic, experiment-based studies examining social systems collect vast amount of data, but often times investigate only the first one or two modes of the distribution of measured variables, and experience difficulty in analyzing the effect of context. For example, in functional diversity research, management teams are described by measures incorporating complex distributions of capabilities of individual managers and teams of managers. To investigate the effect of hidden distributions, and the effect of functional diversity composition on team communication and performance, we developed an agent-based model, and conducted a series of simulation experiments. Modeling results show that depending on the context, such as communication scheme among interacting agents, or their functional composition, intrapersonal functional diversity (IFD), and dominant function diversity (DFD) might enhance or reduce performance and communication among agents. Furthermore, simulation results also suggest that a third measure is required alongside IFD and DFD capturing the aggregate expertise of the team to comprehensively account for empirical findings.

</details>


### [87] [Architectures for Robust Self-Organizing Energy Systems under Information and Control Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21529)
*Emilie Frost,Astrid Nieße*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对基于代理的信息物理能源系统（CPES），提出了考虑信息和行动限制的观察者/控制器架构变体，以实现受控自组织并增强系统对网络攻击等扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络攻击是当前能源系统面临的主要威胁。虽然引入观察者/控制器架构实现受控自组织是保障系统鲁棒性的有效方法，但在实际部署时，必须考虑因本地分布式能源资源隐私、监管约束和数据交换要求而产生的信息获取与行动执行限制。

Method: 提出了考虑信息访问限制和行动限制的观察者与控制器架构变体，并评估了在不同架构下控制器可能采取的行动方案。

Result: 评估结果表明，在设计和实现基于代理的系统时，纳入观察者/控制器架构对于确保其在真实应用场景中的鲁棒性至关重要，证明了所提架构变体的有效性。

Conclusion: 在设计面向实际应用的基于代理的信息物理能源系统时，必须充分考虑观察者/控制器架构及其面临的信息与行动限制，以确保系统的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Applying the concept of controlled self-organization in agent-based Cyber-Physical Energy Systems (CPES) is a promising approach to ensure system robustness. By introducing an observer/controller architecture to the system, this concept allows for self-organization while still enabling intervention when disturbances occur. Thus, it is possible to respond to effects of cyber attacks, a major threat to current energy systems. However, when implementing an observer to monitor the system and a controller to execute actions for controlled self-organization in CPES, it is essential to take into account restrictions on information and actions resulting from the privacy of local distributed energy resources, regulatory constraints, and data exchange requirements. For this reason, this paper presents architecture variants for the observer and controller that take into account restrictions on access to information and limited actions. In addition, it evaluates possible controller actions in various architectures. The results underscore the importance of considering observer/controller architectures when designing agent-based systems to ensure their robustness for real-world applications.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic AI-Enabled Framework for Thermal Comfort and Building Energy Assessment in Tropical Urban Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21787)
*Po-Yen Lai,Xinyu Yang,Derrick Low,Huizhe Liu,Jian Cheng Wong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）与轻量级物理模型的智能推理框架，用于应对新加坡的城市热岛效应与建筑能耗问题，实现了从设计任务解读到物理评估的闭环推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新加坡面临严峻的城市热岛效应和建筑能源需求挑战，需要一种能够综合解读设计任务、提取相关政策并快速进行物理评估的智能方法，以辅助气候适应性建筑设计决策。

Method: 构建了一个代理型AI推理框架，通过提示定制让LLM解读城市设计任务、提取相关政策并激活相应的轻量级物理模型进行评估；这些轻量级模型基于核心热力学与气流原理简化了传统模型，在保证预测精度的同时大幅缩短计算时间，能够预测建筑表面温度、地面辐射热、气流条件等微气候变量以及热舒适度指数（如PET）和建筑能耗。

Result: 该框架使用户能够探索多种气候适应性建筑表面策略（如绿色立面和冷涂料应用），这些策略在改善热舒适度的同时减少了墙体得热和能源需求；系统成功实现了LLM的自主推理能力与轻量级物理模型快速定量评估的结合。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了LLM与物理模型结合在可持续城市设计、室内外环境整合和气候适应规划等跨学科应用中的潜力，为智能辅助建筑设计决策提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: In response to the urban heat island effects and building energy demands in Singapore, this study proposes an agentic AI-enabled reasoning framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with lightweight physics-based models. Through prompt customization, the LLMs interpret urban design tasks, extract relevant policies, and activate appropriate physics-based models for evaluation, forming a closed-loop reasoning-action process. These lightweight physics-based models leverage core thermal and airflow principles, streamlining conventional models to reduce computational time while predicting microclimate variables, such as building surface temperature, ground radiant heat, and airflow conditions, thereby enabling the estimation of thermal comfort indices, e.g., physiological equivalent temperature (PET), and building energy usage. This framework allows users to explore a variety of climate-resilient building surface strategies, e.g., green façades and cool paint applications, that improve thermal comfort while reducing wall heat gain and energy demand. By combining the autonomous reasoning capacity of LLMs with the rapid quantitative evaluation of lightweight physics-based models, the proposed system demonstrates potential for cross-disciplinary applications in sustainable urban design, indoor-outdoor environmental integration, and climate adaptation planning. The source code and data used in this study are available at: https://github.com/PgUpDn/urban-cooling-agent.

</details>

### [89] [Distributed Generative Inference of LLM at Internet Scales with Multi-Dimensional Communication Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21072)
*Jiu Chen,Shuangyan Yang,Xu Xiong,Hexiao Duan,Xinran Zhang,Jie Ren,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BloomBee，一个面向互联网规模的分布式大语言模型推理框架，通过多维度优化通信，将服务吞吐量提升高达1.76倍，平均延迟降低高达43.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化大语言模型推理虽然在异构节点间提供了高性能且成本高效的替代方案，但节点间低网络带宽导致通信成为主要瓶颈，亟需优化。

Method: 提出BloomBee框架，集成大语言模型层分配、微批次处理和张量卸载技术，从多维度优化通信；将这些技术的协调形式化为优化问题并使用动态规划求解；针对低带宽网络定制无损压缩和推测解码以减少通信开销。

Result: 在多种网络环境下评估，BloomBee相比最先进的去中心化大语言模型推理系统，服务吞吐量提升高达1.76倍，平均延迟降低高达43.20%。

Conclusion: BloomBee有效解决了去中心化大语言模型推理中的通信瓶颈问题，显著提升了系统性能，并已开源。

Abstract: Decentralized LLM inference distributes computation among heterogeneous nodes across the internet, offering a performant and cost-efficient solution, alternative to traditional centralized inference. However, the low cross-node network bandwidth makes communication the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we introduce BloomBee, an internet-scale distributed LLM inference framework. BloomBee integrates LLM-layer assignment, micro-batching and tensor offloading to optimize communication from multiple dimensions. Additionally, BloomBee formulates the coordination of these techniques as an optimization problem and solves it using dynamic programming. BloomBee also customizes lossless compression and speculative decoding according to low-bandwidth network settings to reduce communication overhead. We evaluate BloomBee across a spectrum of network environments and show that it improves service throughput by up to 1.76x. It also reduces average latency by up to 43.20% compared to state-of-the-art decentralized LLM inference systems. BloomBee is open-sourced.

</details>


### [90] [TorchGWAS : GPU-accelerated GWAS for thousands of quantitative phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21095)
*Xingzhong Zhao,Ziqian Xie,Islam,Sheikh Muhammad Saiful,Tian Xia,Chen,Cheng,Degui Zhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TorchGWAS是一个基于硬件加速的高通量GWAS框架，在单张NVIDIA A100 GPU上相比传统CPU方法实现了300-1700倍的表型分析吞吐量提升，使得大规模表型筛选变得高效可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生物信息学工作流程（特别是成像和表示学习）可从单个队列生成数千至数万个定量表型。在此类场景下，逐个性状运行全基因组关联分析迅速成为计算瓶颈。现有的GWAS工具虽然对单个性状分析非常有效，但并未针对需要在大型表型面板中重复使用相同基因型矩阵的表型富集筛选工作流程进行优化。

Method: 提出TorchGWAS框架，通过硬件加速实现大型表型面板的高通量关联检验。该框架提供稳定的Python和命令行工作流程，支持线性GWAS和多变量表型筛选，兼容NumPy、PLINK和BGEN基因型输入，能够按样本标识符对齐表型和协变量表，并在内部执行协变量调整。

Result: 在包含890万个标记和23,000个样本的基准测试中，fastGWA在AMD EPYC 7763 64核CPU上每个表型约需100秒，而TorchGWAS在单张NVIDIA A100 GPU上完成2,048个表型仅需10分钟，完成20,480个表型仅需20分钟，相当于表型吞吐量提升约300至1700倍。

Conclusion: TorchGWAS使得在需要高效评估数千个定量性状的表型富集场景下，大规模GWAS筛选变得切实可行，为现代高维表型数据的基因组关联分析提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Motivation: Modern bioinformatics workflows, particularly in imaging and representation learning, can generate thousands to tens of thousands of quantitative phenotypes from a single cohort. In such settings, running genome-wide association analyses trait by trait rapidly becomes a computational bottleneck. While established GWAS tools are highly effective for individual traits, they are not optimized for phenotype-rich screening workflows in which the same genotype matrix is reused across a large phenotype panel. Results: We present TorchGWAS, a framework for high-throughput association testing of large phenotype panels through hardware acceleration. The current public release provides stable Python and command-line workflows for linear GWAS and multivariate phenotype screening, supports NumPy, PLINK, and BGEN genotype inputs, aligns phenotype and covariate tables by sample identifier, and performs covariate adjustment internally. In a benchmark with 8.9 million markers and 23,000 samples, fastGWA required approximately 100 second per phenotype on an AMD EPYC 7763 64-core CPU, whereas TorchGWAS completed 2,048 phenotypes in 10 minute and 20,480 phenotypes in 20 minutes on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, corresponding to an approximately 300- to 1700-fold increase in phenotype throughput. TorchGWAS therefore makes large-scale GWAS screening practical in phenotype-rich settings where thousands of quantitative traits must be evaluated efficiently. Availability and implementation: TorchGWAS is implemented in Python and distributed as a documented source repository at https://github.com/ZhiGroup/TorchGWAS. The current release provides a command-line interface, packaged source code, tutorials, benchmark scripts, and example workflows.

</details>


### [91] [A Cloud-Native Architecture for Human-in-Control LLM-Assisted OpenSearch in Investigative Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21125)
*Benjamin Puhani,Kai Brehmer,Malte Prieß*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向私有云部署的云原生微服务架构，将大语言模型集成到“人在环”工作流中，将自然语言查询转化为OpenSearch DSL，并结合BM25与语义向量的混合检索策略，旨在解决刑事调查中证据量大与语义鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的刑事调查常受限于海量非结构化证据，且自然语言调查意图与技术检索逻辑之间存在语义鸿沟，亟需一种能弥合该差距并满足高安全性与可扩展性要求的解决方案。

Method: 设计并实现一种面向私有云的云原生微服务架构；集成LLM的“Human-in-Control”工作流，将自然语言查询翻译为语法有效的OpenSearch领域特定语言（DSL）；在OpenSearch中实现BM25词法搜索与嵌套语义向量嵌入的混合检索策略。

Result: 通过功能原型验证了技术可行性，建立了系统架构基线，并在受限调查语料库的结构代理——安然邮件数据集上概述了严格的评估方法论，实现了初步功能验证。

Conclusion: 该系统为安全云计算研究提供了贡献，在满足高安全与可扩展需求下利用现代云范式，所提架构为后续实证评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Complex criminal investigations are often hindered by large volumes of unstructured evidence and by the semantic gap between natural language investigative intent and technical search logic. To address this challenge, we present a design and feasibility study of a cloud-native microservice architecture tailored to private-cloud deployments, contributing to research in secure cloud computing and leveraging modern cloud paradigms under high security and scalability requirements. The proposed system integrates Large Language Models into a "Human-in-Control" workflow that translates natural-language queries into syntactically valid OpenSearch Domain-Specific Language expressions. We describe the implementation of a hybrid retrieval strategy within OpenSearch that combines BM25-based lexical search with nested semantic vector embeddings. The paper focuses on system design and preliminary functional validation, establishing an architectural baseline for future empirical evaluation. Technical feasibility is demonstrated through a functional prototype, and a rigorous evaluation methodology is outlined using the Enron Email Dataset as a structural proxy for restricted investigative corpora.

</details>


### [92] [Optimizing High-Throughput Distributed Data Pipelines for Reproducible Deep Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21275)
*Kashish Mittal,Di Yu,Roozbeh Ketabi,Arushi Arora,Brendon Lapp,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模深度学习训练中的数据加载瓶颈，提出了包含下推计算、本地磁盘缓存及确定性队列机制的优化架构，实现了6倍训练加速和严格的数据加载可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数十TB规模的数据集上训练大规模深度学习模型时，面临硬件利用率低和训练难以复现的关键挑战。通过系统分析发现，网络I/O和CPU端的数据转换（如PyArrow转NumPy）导致GPU利用率低至10-15%，严重制约了训练效率。

Method: 提出了优化的数据加载架构：1）采用下推（push-down）机制将数据转换操作移至worker级执行；2）引入Fanout-Cache实现本地磁盘缓存，减少跨epoch的冗余I/O和CPU开销；3）设计了专用的轮询分发队列和结果队列以消除多worker共享队列的竞争条件；4）改进随机数生成器（RNG）处理机制，确保严格确定性的数据加载。

Result: 实验结果表明，优化方案实现了6倍训练加速，端到端训练时间从22小时缩减至3小时；GPU利用率从10-15%提升至超过60%；显著降低了运行间的差异，实现了高吞吐量和可复现的大规模模型训练。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了大规模分布式GPU训练中的数据加载瓶颈问题，通过系统级的优化架构设计，在保证训练可复现性的前提下大幅提升了训练效率和硬件利用率，为大规模深度学习模型训练提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Training massive-scale deep learning models on datasets spanning tens of terabytes presents critical challenges in hardware utilization and training reproducibility. In this paper, we identify and resolve profound data-loading bottlenecks within distributed GPU training pipelines using the Petastorm data loader and Apache Parquet datasets. Through systematic profiling, we demonstrate that network I/O and CPU-bound data transformations (e.g., PyArrow to NumPy) constrain GPU utilization to as low as 10-15%. To address this, we propose an optimized architecture that features push-down worker-level transformations coupled with local-disk caching via Fanout-Cache, minimizing redundant I/O and CPU overhead across training epochs. Furthermore, we eliminate race conditions in multi-worker shared queues by implementing dedicated round-robin ventilator and result queues, alongside modernized RNG handling, achieving strict deterministic data loading. Our optimizations yield a 6x speedup, reducing end-to-end training time from 22 hours to 3 hours, increasing GPU utilization to over 60%, and drastically reducing run-to-run variance, enabling robust, high-throughput, and reproducible large-scale model training.

</details>


### [93] [A Task Decomposition and Planning Framework for Efficient LLM Inference in AI-Enabled WiFi-Offload Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21399)
*Mingqi Han,Xinghua Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用户-边缘协同框架，利用LLM规划器进行任务分解和难度预测，并设计了分解感知调度策略来优化WiFi网络中的LLM推理卸载，相比基准方法平均延迟降低20%，总体奖励提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI WiFi卸载为资源受限无线设备提供大语言模型服务带来了新机遇，但与传统边缘计算不同，LLM推理需要同时解决异构模型能力、无线竞争、任务复杂度不确定性以及推理任务间语义相关性等挑战，亟需一种能够协同处理这些因素的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个用户-边缘协同框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)基于LLM的规划器，不仅执行任务分解，还推断子任务难度和预期输出token长度，从而更准确地估计异构节点上的执行质量和延迟；(2)分解感知调度策略，在通信、排队和计算约束下联合优化子任务分配、执行和聚合。框架支持任务本地执行、直接卸载到边缘AP，或分解为多个子任务协同执行。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提框架在延迟-准确性权衡方面优于本地执行和最近边缘基准方法，平均延迟降低20%，总体奖励提升80%。此外，蒸馏得到的轻量级规划器能够逼近大型教师模型的性能，同时更适合实际边缘部署。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了多用户多边缘WiFi网络中的高效LLM推理卸载，通过智能任务分解和调度策略显著提升了系统性能，证明了用户-边缘协同方法在LLM服务提供中的有效性，轻量化模型设计也为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI WiFi offload is emerging as a promising approach for providing large language model (LLM) services to resource-constrained wireless devices. However, unlike conventional edge computing, LLM inference over WiFi must jointly address heterogeneous model capabilities, wireless contention, uncertain task complexity, and semantic correlation among reasoning tasks. In this paper, we investigate LLM inference offloading in a multi-user multi-edge WiFi network, where each task can be executed locally, directly offloaded to a nearby edge access point (AP), or decomposed into multiple subtasks for collaborative execution across local and edge nodes. To this end, we propose a user-edge collaborative framework with an LLM-based planner that not only performs task decomposition but also infers subtask difficulty and expected output token length, enabling more accurate estimation of execution quality and latency on heterogeneous nodes. Based on these estimates, we further design a decomposition-aware scheduling strategy that jointly optimizes subtask assignment, execution, and aggregation under communication, queuing, and computation constraints. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves a better latency-accuracy tradeoff than local-only and nearest-edge baselines, reducing the average latency by $20\%$ and improving the overall reward by $80\%$. Moreover, the distilled lightweight planner approaches the performance of the large teacher model while remaining more suitable for practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [94] [Research on the efficiency of data loading and storage in Data Lakehouse architectures for the formation of analytical data systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21449)
*Ivan Borodii,Halyna Osukhivska*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次对比评估了Apache Hudi、Apache Iceberg和Delta Lake三种主流Data Lakehouse系统的性能与存储效率。实验表明：Delta Lake在数据加载速度方面表现最优，适合追求快速加载的场景；Apache Iceberg在稳定性和磁盘空间节省方面表现最佳；Apache Hudi在本研究的数据加载与存储评估任务中效果欠佳，但可能在增量更新和流处理场景中具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数据工程师和架构师选择最合适的Data Lakehouse架构提供依据，需理解不同系统在数据加载性能与存储效率之间的权衡，以根据数据类型、数据量及系统需求选择最优方案。

Method: 使用Apache Spark作为分布式数据处理平台，对三种Data Lakehouse系统（Apache Hudi、Apache Iceberg、Delta Lake）进行实证研究。处理结构化（CSV）和半结构化（JSON）数据，数据规模最高达7GB。开发了四个顺序ETL流程，包括读取、转换和加载数据到各系统表中。依据两个关键标准评估效率：数据加载时间和文件系统中表占用的存储空间大小。

Result: Delta Lake在加载任意规模数据时速度最快，是追求数据加载速度优先系统的最优选择。Apache Iceberg在稳定性和磁盘空间节省方面表现最优，适合对存储效率要求高的系统。Apache Hudi在数据加载和存储评估任务中表现不佳，但研究指出其可能在增量更新和流处理场景中具有潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究为选择构建分析数据系统的Data Lakehouse架构提供了实证依据。Delta Lake适合加载速度优先的系统，Apache Iceberg适合稳定性和存储效率优先的系统，Apache Hudi在特定场景（如增量更新、流处理）可能具有应用价值。该研究帮助数据工程师和架构师根据具体需求做出更明智的架构选择。

Abstract: The paper presents a study of the efficiency of loading and storing data in the three most common Data Lakehouse systems, including Apache Hudi, Apache Iceberg, and Delta Lake, using Apache Spark as a distributed data processing platform. The study analyzes the behavior of each system when processing structured (CSV) and semi-structured (JSON) data of different sizes, including loading files up to 7 GB in size. The purpose of the work is to determine the most optimal Data Lakehouse architecture based on the type and volume of data sources, data loading performance using Apache Spark, and disk size of data for forming analytical data systems. The research covers the development of four sequential ETL processes, which include reading, transforming, and loading data into tables in each of the Data Lakehouse systems. The efficiency of each Lakehouse was evaluated according to two key criteria: data loading time and the volume of tables formed in the file system.
